      >> 78:         if (!c.isPrimitive())
      >> 79:             return c;
      >> 80:         if (c == int.class)
      >> 81:             return Integer.class;
      >> 82:         if (c == float.class)
      >> 83:             return Float.class;
      >> 84:         if (c == double.class)
      >> 85:             return Double.class;

      >> 87:             return Long.class;

      >> 89:             return Boolean.class;
      >> 90:         if (c == short.class)
      >> 91:             return Short.class;
      >> 92:         if (c == byte.class)
      >> 93:             return Byte.class;
      >> 94:         if (c == char.class)
      >> 95:             return Character.class;
      >> 96:         return c;
      >> 97:     }

      >> 125:     /**
      >> 126:      * Given two types, return type they should both be converted
      >> 127:      * to before performing any operations between them.
      >> 128:      */
      >> 129:     public static Class<?> promote(Class<?> c1, Class<?> c2) {
      >> 130:         if (c1 == c2)
      >> 131:             return unwrap(c1);
      >> 132:         Class<?> w1 = wrap(c1);
      >> 133:         Class<?> w2 = wrap(c2);
      >> 134:         if (w1 == w2)
      >> 135:             return unwrap(c1);
      >> 136: 
      >> 137:         // not numbers?
      >> 138:         boolean w1Number = Number.class.isAssignableFrom(w1);
      >> 139:         boolean w2Number = Number.class.isAssignableFrom(w2);
      >> 140:         if (!w1Number || !w2Number) {
      >> 141:             // the only non-numeric promotion we do is string to char,
      >> 142:             // or from char/string to number
      >> 143:             if (!w1Number) {
      >> 144:                 if (w2Number && (w1 == Character.class || w1 == String.class))
      >> 145:                     return (w2 == Byte.class || w2 == Short.class)
      >> 146:                         ? Integer.class : unwrap(c2);
      >> 147:                 if (!w2Number && w1 == Character.class && w2 == String.class)
      >> 148:                     return String.class;
      >> 149:                 if (w2Number)
      >> 150:                     return unwrap(c2);
      >> 151:             }
      >> 152:             if (!w2Number) {
      >> 153:                 if (w1Number && (w2 == Character.class || w2 == String.class))
      >> 154:                     return (w1 == Byte.class || w1 == Short.class)
      >> 155:                         ? Integer.class : unwrap(c1);
      >> 156:                 if (!w1Number && w2 == Character.class && w1 == String.class)
      >> 157:                     return String.class;
      >> 158:                 if (w1Number)
      >> 159:                     return unwrap(c1);
      >> 160:             }
      >> 161: 
      >> 162:             // if neither are numbers, use least-derived of the two.  if neither
      >> 163:             // is assignable from the other but one is a standard type, assume
      >> 164:             // the other can be converted to that standard type
      >> 165:             if (!w1Number && !w2Number) {
      >> 166:                 if (w1 == Object.class)
      >> 167:                     return unwrap(c2);
      >> 168:                 if (w2 == Object.class)
      >> 169:                     return unwrap(c1);
      >> 170:                 if (w1.isAssignableFrom(w2))
      >> 171:                     return unwrap(c1);
      >> 172:                 if (w2.isAssignableFrom(w1))
      >> 173:                     return unwrap(c2);
      >> 174:                 if (isNonstandardType(w1))
      >> 175:                     return (isNonstandardType(w2)) ? Object.class : unwrap(c2);
      >> 176:                 if (isNonstandardType(w2))
      >> 177:                     return (isNonstandardType(w1)) ? Object.class : unwrap(c1);
      >> 178:             }
      >> 179:             return Object.class;
      >> 180:         }
      >> 181: 
      >> 182:         if (w1 == BigDecimal.class || w2 == BigDecimal.class)
      >> 183:             return BigDecimal.class;
      >> 184:         if (w1 == BigInteger.class) {
      >> 185:             if (w2 == Float.class || w2 == Double.class)
      >> 186:                 return BigDecimal.class;
      >> 187:             return BigInteger.class;
      >> 188:         }
      >> 189:         if (w2 == BigInteger.class) {
      >> 190:             if (w1 == Float.class || w1 == Double.class)
      >> 191:                 return BigDecimal.class;
      >> 192:             return BigInteger.class;
      >> 193:         }
      >> 194:         if (w1 == Double.class || w2 == Double.class)
      >> 195:             return double.class;
      >> 196:         if (w1 == Float.class || w2 == Float.class)
      >> 197:             return float.class;
      >> 198:         if (w1 == Long.class || w2 == Long.class)
      >> 199:             return long.class;
      >> 200:         return int.class;
      >> 201:     }

      >> 206:     private static boolean isNonstandardType(Class<?> c) {

      >> 214:         case JavaTypes.OID:

      >> 216:             return true;

      >> 218:             return false;

      >> 226:     public static boolean canConvert(Class<?> c1, Class<?> c2, boolean strict) {
      >> 227:         if (c1 == c2)
      >> 228:             return true;
      >> 229:         c1 = wrap(c1);
      >> 230:         c2 = wrap(c2);
      >> 231:         if (c2.isAssignableFrom(c1))
      >> 232:             return true;
      >> 233: 
      >> 234:         boolean c1Number = Number.class.isAssignableFrom(c1);
      >> 235:         boolean c2Number = Number.class.isAssignableFrom(c2);
      >> 236:         if (c1Number && c2Number)
      >> 237:             return true;
      >> 238:         if ((c1Number && (c2 == Character.class
      >> 239:             || (!strict && c2 == String.class)))
      >> 240:             || (c2Number && (c1 == Character.class
      >> 241:             || (!strict && c1 == String.class))))
      >> 242:             return true;
      >> 243:         if (c1 == String.class && c2 == Character.class)
      >> 244:             return true;
      >> 245:         if (c2 == String.class)
      >> 246:             return !strict;
      >> 247:         if (c1 == String.class && isTemporalType(c2))
      >> 248:             return true;
      >> 249:         if ((c1 == java.util.Date.class ||c1 == java.sql.Time.class) && c2 == java.sql.Timestamp.class)
      >> 250:             return false;
      >> 251:         if ((c1 == java.util.Date.class ||c1 == java.sql.Timestamp.class) && c2 == java.sql.Time.class)
      >> 252:             return false;
      >> 253:         if (isTemporalType(c1) && isTemporalType(c2))
      >> 254:             return true;
      >> 255:         return false;
      >> 256:     }

      >> 277:     /**
      >> 278:      * Convert the given value to the given type.
      >> 279:      * @param o the given value
      >> 280:      * @param type the target type
      >> 281:      */
      >> 282:     public static Object convert(Object o, Class<?> type, boolean strictNumericConversion) {
      >> 283:         if (o == null)
      >> 284:             return null;
      >> 285:         if (o.getClass() == type)
      >> 286:             return o;
      >> 287: 
      >> 288:         type = wrap(type);
      >> 289:         if (type.isAssignableFrom(o.getClass()))
      >> 290:             return o;
      >> 291: 
      >> 292:         // the non-numeric conversions we do are to string, or from
      >> 293:         // string/char to number, or calendar/date
      >> 294:         // String to Boolean
      >> 295:         // String to Integer
      >> 296:         boolean num = o instanceof Number;
      >> 297:         if (!num) {
      >> 298:             if (type == String.class) {
      >> 299:                 return o.toString();
      >> 300:             }
      >> 301:             else if (type == Boolean.class && o instanceof String) {
      >> 302:                 return Boolean.valueOf(o.toString());
      >> 303:             }
      >> 304:             else if (type == Integer.class && o instanceof String) {
      >> 305:                 try {
      >> 306:                     return new Integer(o.toString());
      >> 307:                 }
      >> 308:                 catch (NumberFormatException e) {
      >> 309:                     throw new ClassCastException(_loc.get("cant-convert", o, o.getClass(), type).getMessage());
      >> 310:                 }
      >> 311:             }
      >> 312:             else if (type == Character.class) {
      >> 313:                 String str = o.toString();
      >> 314:                 if (str != null && str.length() == 1) {
      >> 315:                     return str.charAt(0);
      >> 316:                 }
      >> 317:             }
      >> 318:             else if (Calendar.class.isAssignableFrom(type) && o instanceof Date) {
      >> 319:                 Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
      >> 320:                 cal.setTime((Date) o);
      >> 321:                 return cal;
      >> 322:             }
      >> 323:             else if (Date.class.isAssignableFrom(type) && o instanceof Calendar) {
      >> 324:                 return ((Calendar) o).getTime();
      >> 325:             }
      >> 326:             else if (Number.class.isAssignableFrom(type)) {
      >> 327:                 Integer i = null;
      >> 328:                 if (o instanceof Character) {
      >> 329:                     i = Integer.valueOf((Character) o);
      >> 330:                 }
      >> 331:                 else if (o instanceof String && ((String) o).length() == 1) {
      >> 332:                     i = Integer.valueOf(((String) o));
      >> 333:                 }
      >> 334: 
      >> 335:                 if (i != null) {
      >> 336:                     if (type == Integer.class) {
      >> 337:                         return i;
      >> 338:                     }
      >> 339:                     num = true;
      >> 340:                 }
      >> 341:             } else if (Temporal.class.isAssignableFrom(type)) {
      >> 342:                 // handling of Java8 time API.
      >> 343:                 if (LocalDate.class.equals(type)) {
      >> 344:                     if (o instanceof java.sql.Date) {
      >> 345:                         return ((java.sql.Date) o).toLocalDate();
      >> 346:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {
      >> 347:                         return new java.sql.Date(((java.util.Date)o).getTime()).toLocalDate();
      >> 348:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 349:                         return LocalDate.parse((CharSequence) o);
      >> 350:                     }
      >> 351:                 } else if (LocalDateTime.class.equals(type)) {
      >> 352:                     if (o instanceof java.sql.Timestamp) {
      >> 353:                         return ((java.sql.Timestamp) o).toLocalDateTime();
      >> 354:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {
      >> 355:                         return ((java.util.Date)o).toInstant().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toLocalDateTime();
      >> 356:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 357:                         return LocalDateTime.parse((CharSequence) o);
      >> 358:                     }
      >> 359:                 } else if (LocalTime.class.equals(type)) {
      >> 360:                     if (o instanceof java.sql.Time) {
      >> 361:                         return ((java.sql.Time) o).toLocalTime();
      >> 362:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {
      >> 363:                         return ((java.util.Date)o).toInstant().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toLocalTime();
      >> 364:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 365:                         return LocalTime.parse((CharSequence) o);

      >> 378:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {

      >> 380:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 381:                         return OffsetDateTime.parse((CharSequence) o);
      >> 382:                     }
      >> 383:                 }
      >> 384: 
      >> 385:             } else if (o instanceof String && isJDBCTemporalSyntax(o.toString())) {
      >> 386:                 try {
      >> 387:                     Object temporal = parseJDBCTemporalSyntax(o.toString());
      >> 388:                     if (temporal != null && type.isAssignableFrom(temporal.getClass()))
      >> 389:                         return temporal;
      >> 390:                 } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
      >> 391: 
      >> 392:                 }
      >> 393:             } else if (o instanceof String && type.isEnum()) {
      >> 394:                 return Enum.valueOf((Class<Enum>)type, o.toString());
      >> 395:             }
      >> 396:         }
      >> 397:         if (!num) {
      >> 398:             throw new ClassCastException(_loc.get("cant-convert", o, o.getClass(), type).getMessage());
      >> 399:         }
      >> 400: 
      >> 401:         if (type == Integer.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 402:             return ((Number) o).intValue();
      >> 403:         } else if (type == Float.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 404:             return ((Number) o).floatValue();
      >> 405:         } else if (type == Double.class) {
      >> 406:             return ((Number) o).doubleValue();
      >> 407:         } else if (type == Long.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 408:             return ((Number) o).longValue();
      >> 409:         } else if (type == BigDecimal.class) {
      >> 410:             // the BigDecimal constructor doesn't handle the
      >> 411:             // "NaN" string version of Double.NaN and Float.NaN, nor
      >> 412:             // does it handle infinity; we need to instead use the Double
      >> 413:             // and Float versions, despite wanting to cast it to BigDecimal
      >> 414:             double dval = ((Number) o).doubleValue();
      >> 415:             if (Double.isNaN(dval) || Double.isInfinite(dval)) {
      >> 416:                 return dval;
      >> 417:             }
      >> 418:             float fval = ((Number) o).floatValue();
      >> 419:             if (Float.isNaN(fval) || Float.isInfinite(fval)) {
      >> 420:                 return fval;
      >> 421:             }
      >> 422: 
      >> 423:             return new BigDecimal(o.toString());
      >> 424:         } else if (type == BigInteger.class) {
      >> 425:             return new BigInteger(o.toString());
      >> 426:         } else if (type == Short.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 427:             return ((Number) o).shortValue();
      >> 428:         } else if (type == Byte.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 429:             return ((Number) o).byteValue();
      >> 430:         } else if (type == Character.class) {
      >> 431:             return (char) ((Number) o).intValue();
      >> 432:         } else if (!strictNumericConversion) {
      >> 433:             return ((Number) o).intValue();
      >> 434:         } else {
      >> 435:             throw new ClassCastException(_loc.get("cant-convert", o, o.getClass(), type).getMessage());
      >> 436:         }
      >> 437:     }
      >> 438: 
      >> 439:     private static boolean allowNumericConversion(Class<?> actual, Class<?> target, boolean strict) {
      >> 440:         if (!strict || actual == target)
      >> 441:             return true;
      >> 442:         if (actual == Byte.class)    return false;
      >> 443:         if (actual == Double.class)  return target == Float.class;
      >> 444:         if (actual == Float.class)   return target == Double.class;
      >> 445:         if (actual == Integer.class) return target == Long.class || target == Short.class;
      >> 446:         if (actual == Long.class)    return target == Integer.class || target == Short.class;
      >> 447:         if (actual == Short.class)   return target == Long.class || target == Integer.class;
      >> 448:         return false;
      >> 449:     }

      >> 497:     /**
      >> 498:      * Perform the given operation on two numbers.
      >> 499:      */
      >> 500:     private static Object op(Object o1, Class<?> c1, Object o2, Class<?> c2, int op) {
      >> 501:         Class<?> promote = promote(c1, c2);
      >> 502:         if (promote == int.class) {
      >> 503:             int n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0 : ((Number) o1).intValue();
      >> 504:             int n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0 : ((Number) o2).intValue();
      >> 505:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 506:         }
      >> 507:         if (promote == float.class) {
      >> 508:             float n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0F : ((Number) o1).floatValue();
      >> 509:             float n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0F : ((Number) o2).floatValue();
      >> 510:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 511:         }
      >> 512:         if (promote == double.class) {
      >> 513:             double n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0D : ((Number) o1).doubleValue();
      >> 514:             double n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0D : ((Number) o2).doubleValue();
      >> 515:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 516:         }
      >> 517:         if (promote == long.class) {
      >> 518:             long n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0L : ((Number) o1).longValue();
      >> 519:             long n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0L : ((Number) o2).longValue();
      >> 520:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 521:         }
      >> 522:         if (promote == BigDecimal.class) {
      >> 523:             BigDecimal n1 = (o1 == null) ? ZERO_BIGDECIMAL
      >> 524:                 : (BigDecimal) convert(o1, promote);
      >> 525:             BigDecimal n2 = (o2 == null) ? ZERO_BIGDECIMAL
      >> 526:                 : (BigDecimal) convert(o2, promote);
      >> 527:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 528:         }
      >> 529:         if (promote == BigInteger.class) {
      >> 530:             BigInteger n1 = (o1 == null) ? ZERO_BIGINTEGER
      >> 531:                 : (BigInteger) convert(o1, promote);
      >> 532:             BigInteger n2 = (o2 == null) ? ZERO_BIGINTEGER
      >> 533:                 : (BigInteger) convert(o2, promote);
      >> 534:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 535:         }
      >> 536:         // default to int
      >> 537:         int n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0 : ((Number) o1).intValue();
      >> 538:         int n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0 : ((Number) o2).intValue();
      >> 539:         return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 540:     }
      >> 541: 
      >> 542:     /**
      >> 543:      * Return the result of a mathematical operation.
      >> 544:      */
      >> 545:     private static Object op(int n1, int n2, int op) {
      >> 546:         int tot;
      >> 547:         switch (op) {
      >> 548:             case OP_ADD:
      >> 549:                 tot = n1 + n2;
      >> 550:                 break;
      >> 551:             case OP_SUBTRACT:
      >> 552:                 tot = n1 - n2;
      >> 553:                 break;
      >> 554:             case OP_MULTIPLY:
      >> 555:                 tot = n1 * n2;
      >> 556:                 break;
      >> 557:             case OP_DIVIDE:
      >> 558:                 tot = n1 / n2;
      >> 559:                 break;
      >> 560:             case OP_MOD:
      >> 561:                 tot = n1 % n2;
      >> 562:                 break;
      >> 563:             case OP_POWER:
      >> 564:                 return Math.pow(n1, n2);
      >> 565:             case OP_ROUND:
      >> 566:                 tot = n1;
      >> 567:             default:
      >> 568:                 throw new InternalException();
      >> 569:         }
      >> 570:         return tot;
      >> 571:     }
      >> 572: 
      >> 573:     /**
      >> 574:      * Return the result of a mathematical operation.
      >> 575:      */
      >> 576:     private static Object op(float n1, float n2, int op) {
      >> 577:         float tot;
      >> 578:         switch (op) {
      >> 579:             case OP_ADD:
      >> 580:                 tot = n1 + n2;
      >> 581:                 break;
      >> 582:             case OP_SUBTRACT:
      >> 583:                 tot = n1 - n2;
      >> 584:                 break;
      >> 585:             case OP_MULTIPLY:
      >> 586:                 tot = n1 * n2;
      >> 587:                 break;
      >> 588:             case OP_DIVIDE:
      >> 589:                 tot = n1 / n2;
      >> 590:                 break;
      >> 591:             case OP_MOD:
      >> 592:                 tot = n1 % n2;
      >> 593:                 break;
      >> 594:             case OP_POWER:
      >> 595:                 return Math.pow(n1, n2);
      >> 596:             case OP_ROUND:
      >> 597:                 BigDecimal bg = new BigDecimal(Float.toString(n1));
      >> 598:                 return bg.setScale(Math.toIntExact(Math.round(n2)), RoundingMode.HALF_EVEN).floatValue();
      >> 599:         default:
      >> 600:                 throw new InternalException();
      >> 601:         }
      >> 602:         return tot;
      >> 603:     }

      >> 669:     /**
      >> 670:      * Return the result of a mathematical operation.
      >> 671:      */
      >> 672:     private static Object op(BigDecimal n1, BigDecimal n2, int op) {
      >> 673:         switch (op) {
      >> 674:             case OP_ADD:
      >> 675:                 return n1.add(n2);
      >> 676:             case OP_SUBTRACT:
      >> 677:                 return n1.subtract(n2);
      >> 678:             case OP_MULTIPLY:
      >> 679:                 return n1.multiply(n2);
      >> 680:             case OP_DIVIDE:
      >> 681:                 int scale = Math.max(n1.scale(), n2.scale());
      >> 682:                 return n1.divide(n2, scale, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_UP);
      >> 683:             case OP_MOD:
      >> 684:                 throw new UserException(_loc.get("mod-bigdecimal"));
      >> 685:             case OP_POWER:
      >> 686:                 return n1.pow(n2.intValue());
      >> 687:             case OP_ROUND:
      >> 688:                 return n1.setScale(n2.intValue(), RoundingMode.HALF_EVEN);
      >> 689:             default:
      >> 690:                 throw new InternalException();
      >> 691:         }
      >> 692:     }
      >> 693: 
      >> 694:     /**
      >> 695:      * Return the result of a mathematical operation.
      >> 696:      */
      >> 697:     private static Object op(BigInteger n1, BigInteger n2, int op) {
      >> 698:         switch (op) {
      >> 699:             case OP_ADD:
      >> 700:                 return n1.add(n2);
      >> 701:             case OP_SUBTRACT:
      >> 702:                 return n1.subtract(n2);
      >> 703:             case OP_MULTIPLY:
      >> 704:                 return n1.multiply(n2);
      >> 705:             case OP_DIVIDE:
      >> 706:                 return n1.divide(n2);
      >> 707:             case OP_POWER:
      >> 708:                 return n1.pow(n2.intValue());
      >> 709:             case OP_ROUND:
      >> 710:                 return n1;
      >> 711:             default:
      >> 712:                 throw new InternalException();
      >> 713:         }
      >> 714:     }
      >> 715: 
      >> 716:     /**
      >> 717:      * Parses the given declarations into a list of type, name, type, name...
      >> 718:      * Returns null if no declarations. Assumes declaration is not an empty
      >> 719:      * string and is already trimmed (valid assumptions given the checks made
      >> 720:      * in our setters).
      >> 721:      *
      >> 722:      * @param decType the type of declaration being parsed, for use in
      >> 723:      * error messages
      >> 724:      */
      >> 725:     public static List<String> parseDeclaration(String dec, char split, String decType) {
      >> 726:         if (dec == null)
      >> 727:             return null;
      >> 728: 
      >> 729:         // watch for common mixups between commas and semis
      >> 730:         char bad = (char) 0;
      >> 731:         if (split == ',')
      >> 732:             bad = ';';
      >> 733:         else if (split == ';')
      >> 734:             bad = ',';
      >> 735: 
      >> 736:         char sentinal = ' ';
      >> 737:         char cur;
      >> 738:         int start = 0;
      >> 739:         boolean skipSpace = false;
      >> 740:         List<String> results = new ArrayList<>(6);
      >> 741:         for (int i = 0; i < dec.length(); i++) {
      >> 742:             cur = dec.charAt(i);
      >> 743:             if (cur == bad)
      >> 744:                 throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-dec", dec, decType));
      >> 745:             if (cur == ' ' && skipSpace) {
      >> 746:                 start++;
      >> 747:                 continue;
      >> 748:             }
      >> 749: 
      >> 750:             skipSpace = false;
      >> 751:             if (cur != sentinal)
      >> 752:                 continue;
      >> 753: 
      >> 754:             // if looking for spaces, look for split char, or vice versa
      >> 755:             sentinal = (sentinal == ' ') ? split : ' ';
      >> 756:             results.add(dec.substring(start, i).trim());
      >> 757:             start = i + 1;
      >> 758:             skipSpace = true;
      >> 759:         }
      >> 760: 
      >> 761:         // add last token, if any
      >> 762:         if (start < dec.length())
      >> 763:             results.add(dec.substring(start));
      >> 764: 
      >> 765:         // if not an even number of elements, something is wrong
      >> 766:         if (results.isEmpty() || results.size() % 2 != 0)
      >> 767:             throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-dec", dec, decType));
      >> 768: 
      >> 769:         return results;
      >> 770:     }
      >> 771: 
      >> 772:     /**
      >> 773:      * Split the given expression list into distinct expressions. Assumes the
      >> 774:      * given string is not null or of zero length and is already trimmed
      >> 775:      * (valid assumptions given the checks in our setters and before
      >> 776:      * this method call).
      >> 777:      */
      >> 778:     public static List<String> splitExpressions(String str, char split, int expected) {
      >> 779:         if (str == null)
      >> 780:             return null;
      >> 781: 
      >> 782:         List<String> exps = null;
      >> 783:         int parenDepth = 0;
      >> 784:         int begin = 0, pos = 0;
      >> 785:         boolean escape = false;
      >> 786:         boolean string = false;
      >> 787:         boolean nonspace = false;
      >> 788:         char quote = 0;
      >> 789:         for (char c; pos < str.length(); pos++) {
      >> 790:             c = str.charAt(pos);
      >> 791:             if (c == '\\') {
      >> 792:                 escape = !escape;
      >> 793:                 continue;
      >> 794:             }
      >> 795:             if (escape) {
      >> 796:                 escape = false;
      >> 797:                 continue;
      >> 798:             }
      >> 799: 
      >> 800:             switch (c) {
      >> 801:                 case '\'':
      >> 802:                 case '"':
      >> 803:                     if (string && quote == c)
      >> 804:                         string = false;
      >> 805:                     else if (!string) {
      >> 806:                         quote = c;
      >> 807:                         string = true;
      >> 808:                     }
      >> 809:                     nonspace = true;
      >> 810:                     break;
      >> 811:                 case '(':
      >> 812:                     if (!string)
      >> 813:                         parenDepth++;
      >> 814:                     nonspace = true;
      >> 815:                     break;
      >> 816:                 case ')':
      >> 817:                     if (!string)
      >> 818:                         parenDepth--;
      >> 819:                     nonspace = true;
      >> 820:                     break;
      >> 821:                 case ' ':
      >> 822:                 case '\t':
      >> 823:                 case '\n':
      >> 824:                 case '\r':
      >> 825:                     if (c == split && !string && parenDepth == 0 && nonspace) {
      >> 826:                         if (exps == null)
      >> 827:                             exps = new ArrayList<>(expected);
      >> 828:                         exps.add(str.substring(begin, pos).trim());
      >> 829:                         begin = pos + 1;
      >> 830:                         nonspace = false;
      >> 831:                     }
      >> 832:                     break;
      >> 833:                 default:
      >> 834:                     if (c == split && !string && parenDepth == 0) {
      >> 835:                         if (exps == null)
      >> 836:                             exps = new ArrayList<>(expected);
      >> 837:                         exps.add(str.substring(begin, pos).trim());
      >> 838:                         begin = pos + 1;
      >> 839:                     }
      >> 840:                     nonspace = true;
      >> 841:             }
      >> 842:             escape = false;
      >> 843:         }
      >> 844: 
      >> 845:         if (exps == null) {
      >> 846:             exps = Collections.singletonList(str);
      >> 847:             return exps;
      >> 848:         }
      >> 849: 
      >> 850:         // add last expression and return array
      >> 851:         String last = str.substring(begin).trim();
      >> 852:         if (last.length() > 0)
      >> 853:             exps.add(last);
      >> 854:         return exps;
      >> 855:     }
      >> 856: 
      >> 857:     /**
      >> 858:      * Add the given access path metadatas to the full path list, making sure
      >> 859:      * to maintain only base metadatas in the list. The given list may be null.
      >> 860:      */
      >> 861:     public static List<ClassMetaData> addAccessPathMetaDatas(List<ClassMetaData> metas, ClassMetaData[] path) {
      >> 862:         if (path == null || path.length == 0)
      >> 863:             return metas;
      >> 864: 
      >> 865:         // create set of base class metadatas in access path
      >> 866:         if (metas == null)
      >> 867:             metas = new ArrayList<>();
      >> 868:         int last = metas.size();
      >> 869: 
      >> 870:         // for every element in the path of this executor, compare it
      >> 871:         // to already-gathered elements to see if it should replace
      >> 872:         // a subclass in the list or should be added as a new base;
      >> 873:         // at least it's n^2 of a small n...
      >> 874:         ClassMetaData meta;
      >> 875:         boolean add;
      >> 876:         for (ClassMetaData classMetaData : path) {
      >> 877:             add = true;
      >> 878:             for (int j = 0; add && j < last; j++) {
      >> 879:                 meta = metas.get(j);
      >> 880: 
      >> 881:                 if (meta.getDescribedType().isAssignableFrom(classMetaData.getDescribedType())) {
      >> 882:                     // list already contains base class
      >> 883:                     add = false;
      >> 884:                 }
      >> 885:                 else if (classMetaData.getDescribedType().isAssignableFrom(meta.getDescribedType())) {
      >> 886:                     // this element replaces its subclass
      >> 887:                     add = false;
      >> 888:                     metas.set(j, classMetaData);
      >> 889:                 }
      >> 890:             }
      >> 891: 
      >> 892:             // if no base class of current path element already in
      >> 893:             // list and path element didn't replace a subclass in the
      >> 894:             // list, then add it now as a new base
      >> 895:             if (add)
      >> 896:                 metas.add(classMetaData);
      >> 897:         }
      >> 898:         return metas;
      >> 899:     }
      >> 900: 
      >> 901:     /**
      >> 902:      * Convert the user-given hint value to an aggregate listener.
      >> 903:      * The hint can be an aggregate listener instance or class name.
      >> 904:      */
      >> 905:     public static AggregateListener hintToAggregateListener(Object hint, ClassLoader loader) {
      >> 906:         if (hint == null)
      >> 907:             return null;
      >> 908:         if (hint instanceof AggregateListener)
      >> 909:             return (AggregateListener) hint;
      >> 910: 
      >> 911:         Exception cause = null;
      >> 912:         if (hint instanceof String) {
      >> 913:             try {
      >> 914:                 return (AggregateListener) AccessController.doPrivileged(
      >> 915:                     J2DoPrivHelper.newInstanceAction(Class.forName((String) hint, true, loader)));
      >> 916:             } catch (Exception e) {
      >> 917:                 if (e instanceof PrivilegedActionException)
      >> 918:                     e = ((PrivilegedActionException) e).getException();
      >> 919:                 cause = e;
      >> 920:             }
      >> 921:         }
      >> 922:         throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-agg-listener-hint", hint,
      >> 923:             hint.getClass())).setCause(cause);
      >> 924:     }
      >> 925: 
      >> 926:     /**
      >> 927:      * Convert the user-given hint value to an array of aggregate listeners.
      >> 928:      * The hint can be an aggregate listener, aggregate listener array,
      >> 929:      * collection, or comma-separated class names.
      >> 930:      */
      >> 931:     public static AggregateListener[] hintToAggregateListeners(Object hint, ClassLoader loader) {
      >> 932:         if (hint == null)
      >> 933:             return null;
      >> 934:         if (hint instanceof AggregateListener[])
      >> 935:             return (AggregateListener[]) hint;
      >> 936:         if (hint instanceof AggregateListener)
      >> 937:             return new AggregateListener[]{ (AggregateListener) hint };
      >> 938:         if (hint instanceof Collection) {
      >> 939:             Collection<AggregateListener> c = (Collection<AggregateListener>) hint;
      >> 940:             return c.toArray(new AggregateListener[c.size()]);
      >> 941:         }
      >> 942: 
      >> 943:         Exception cause = null;
      >> 944:         if (hint instanceof String) {
      >> 945:             String[] clss = StringUtil.split((String) hint, ",", 0);
      >> 946:             AggregateListener[] aggs = new AggregateListener[clss.length];
      >> 947:             try {
      >> 948:                 for (int i = 0; i < clss.length; i++)
      >> 949:                     aggs[i] = (AggregateListener) AccessController.doPrivileged(
      >> 950:                         J2DoPrivHelper.newInstanceAction(
      >> 951:                             Class.forName(clss[i], true, loader)));
      >> 952:                 return aggs;
      >> 953:             } catch (Exception e) {
      >> 954:                 if (e instanceof PrivilegedActionException)
      >> 955:                     e = ((PrivilegedActionException) e).getException();
      >> 956:                 cause = e;
      >> 957:             }
      >> 958:         }
      >> 959:         throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-agg-listener-hint", hint,
      >> 960:             hint.getClass())).setCause(cause);
      >> 961:     }
      >> 962:

      >> 1026:     /**
      >> 1027:      * Return the value of the property named by the hint key.
      >> 1028:      */
      >> 1029:     public static Object hintToGetter(Object target, String hintKey) {
      >> 1030:         if (target == null || hintKey == null)
      >> 1031:             return null;
      >> 1032: 
      >> 1033:         Method getter = Reflection.findGetter(target.getClass(), hintKey, true);
      >> 1034:         return Reflection.get(target, getter);
      >> 1035:     }
      >> 1036: 
      >> 1037:     /**
      >> 1038:      * Set the value of the property named by the hint key.
      >> 1039:      */
      >> 1040:     public static void hintToSetter(Object target, String hintKey, Object value) {
      >> 1041:         if (target == null || hintKey == null)
      >> 1042:             return;
      >> 1043: 
      >> 1044:         Method setter = Reflection.findSetter(target.getClass(), hintKey, true);
      >> 1045:         if (value instanceof String) {
      >> 1046:             if ("null".equals(value))
      >> 1047:                 value = null;
      >> 1048:             else {
      >> 1049:                 try {
      >> 1050:                     value = StringUtil.parse((String) value, setter.getParameterTypes()[0]);
      >> 1051:                 } catch (Exception e) {
      >> 1052:                     throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-setter-hint-arg",
      >> 1053:                         hintKey, value, setter.getParameterTypes()[0])).
      >> 1054:                         setCause(e);
      >> 1055:                 }
      >> 1056:             }
      >> 1057:         }
      >> 1058:         Reflection.set(target, setter, value);
      >> 1059:     }
      >> 1060: 
      >> 1061:     /**
      >> 1062:      * Parses the given string assuming it is a JDBC key expression. Extracts the
      >> 1063:      * data portion and based on the key, calls static java.sql.Date/Time/Timestamp.valueOf(String)
      >> 1064:      * method to convert to a java.sql.Date/Time/Timestamp instance.
      >> 1065:      */
      >> 1066:     public static Object parseJDBCTemporalSyntax(String s) {
      >> 1067:         s = clip(s.trim(), "{", "}", true);
      >> 1068:         if (s.startsWith("ts")) {
      >> 1069:             return java.sql.Timestamp.valueOf(clip(s.substring(2).trim(), "'", "'", false));
      >> 1070:         } else if (s.startsWith("d")) {
      >> 1071:             return java.sql.Date.valueOf(clip(s.substring(1).trim(), "'", "'", false));
      >> 1072:         } else if (s.startsWith("t")) {
      >> 1073:             return java.sql.Time.valueOf(clip(s.substring(2).trim(), "'", "'", false));
      >> 1074:         } else {
      >> 1075:             return null;
      >> 1076:         }
      >> 1077:     }

      >> 1079:     /**
      >> 1080:      * Affirms if the given String is enclosed in {}.
      >> 1081:      *
      >> 1082:      */
      >> 1083:     public static boolean isJDBCTemporalSyntax(String s) {
      >> 1084:         if (s != null) {
      >> 1085:             s = s.trim();
      >> 1086:         }
      >> 1087:         return s != null && s.startsWith("{") && s.endsWith("}");

      >> 1090:     /**
      >> 1091:      * Removes the first and last string if they are the terminal sequence in the given string.
      >> 1092:      *
      >> 1093:      * @param s a string to be examined
      >> 1094:      * @param first the characters in the beginning of the given string
      >> 1095:      * @param last the characters in the end of the given string
      >> 1096:      * @param fail if true throws exception if the given string does not have the given terminal sequences.
      >> 1097:      * @return the string with terminal sequences removed.
      >> 1098:      */
      >> 1099:     public static String clip(String s, String first, String last, boolean fail) {
      >> 1100:         if (s == null)
      >> 1101:             return s;
      >> 1102:         if (s.startsWith(first) && s.endsWith(last)) {
      >> 1103:             return s.substring(first.length(), s.length()-last.length()).trim();
      >> 1104:         }
      >> 1105:         if (fail) {
      >> 1106:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(s + " is not valid escape syntax for JDBC");
      >> 1107:         }
      >> 1108:         return s;
      >> 1109:     }
      >> 1110: 
      >> 1111:     /**
      >> 1112:      * Affirms if the given class is Data, Time or Timestamp.
      >> 1113:      */
      >> 1114:     public static boolean isTemporalType(Class<?> c) {
      >> 1115:         return c != null
      >> 1116:             && (Date.class.isAssignableFrom(c)
      >> 1117:              || Time.class.isAssignableFrom(c)
      >> 1118:              || Timestamp.class.isAssignableFrom(c)
      >> 1119:              || Calendar.class.isAssignableFrom(c)
      >> 1120:              || LocalDate.class == c  // java.time classes are final, so we can compare with ==
      >> 1121:              || LocalDateTime.class == c
      >> 1122:              || LocalTime.class == c
      >> 1123:              || OffsetTime.class ==c
      >> 1124:              || OffsetDateTime.class == c);
      >> 1125:     }

      >> 125:     /**
      >> 126:      * Given two types, return type they should both be converted
      >> 127:      * to before performing any operations between them.
      >> 128:      */
      >> 129:     public static Class<?> promote(Class<?> c1, Class<?> c2) {
      >> 130:         if (c1 == c2)
      >> 131:             return unwrap(c1);
      >> 132:         Class<?> w1 = wrap(c1);
      >> 133:         Class<?> w2 = wrap(c2);
      >> 134:         if (w1 == w2)
      >> 135:             return unwrap(c1);
      >> 136: 
      >> 137:         // not numbers?
      >> 138:         boolean w1Number = Number.class.isAssignableFrom(w1);
      >> 139:         boolean w2Number = Number.class.isAssignableFrom(w2);
      >> 140:         if (!w1Number || !w2Number) {
      >> 141:             // the only non-numeric promotion we do is string to char,
      >> 142:             // or from char/string to number
      >> 143:             if (!w1Number) {
      >> 144:                 if (w2Number && (w1 == Character.class || w1 == String.class))
      >> 145:                     return (w2 == Byte.class || w2 == Short.class)
      >> 146:                         ? Integer.class : unwrap(c2);
      >> 147:                 if (!w2Number && w1 == Character.class && w2 == String.class)
      >> 148:                     return String.class;
      >> 149:                 if (w2Number)
      >> 150:                     return unwrap(c2);
      >> 151:             }
      >> 152:             if (!w2Number) {
      >> 153:                 if (w1Number && (w2 == Character.class || w2 == String.class))
      >> 154:                     return (w1 == Byte.class || w1 == Short.class)
      >> 155:                         ? Integer.class : unwrap(c1);
      >> 156:                 if (!w1Number && w2 == Character.class && w1 == String.class)
      >> 157:                     return String.class;
      >> 158:                 if (w1Number)
      >> 159:                     return unwrap(c1);
      >> 160:             }
      >> 161: 
      >> 162:             // if neither are numbers, use least-derived of the two.  if neither
      >> 163:             // is assignable from the other but one is a standard type, assume
      >> 164:             // the other can be converted to that standard type
      >> 165:             if (!w1Number && !w2Number) {
      >> 166:                 if (w1 == Object.class)
      >> 167:                     return unwrap(c2);
      >> 168:                 if (w2 == Object.class)
      >> 169:                     return unwrap(c1);
      >> 170:                 if (w1.isAssignableFrom(w2))
      >> 171:                     return unwrap(c1);
      >> 172:                 if (w2.isAssignableFrom(w1))
      >> 173:                     return unwrap(c2);
      >> 174:                 if (isNonstandardType(w1))
      >> 175:                     return (isNonstandardType(w2)) ? Object.class : unwrap(c2);
      >> 176:                 if (isNonstandardType(w2))
      >> 177:                     return (isNonstandardType(w1)) ? Object.class : unwrap(c1);
      >> 178:             }
      >> 179:             return Object.class;
      >> 180:         }
      >> 181: 
      >> 182:         if (w1 == BigDecimal.class || w2 == BigDecimal.class)
      >> 183:             return BigDecimal.class;
      >> 184:         if (w1 == BigInteger.class) {
      >> 185:             if (w2 == Float.class || w2 == Double.class)
      >> 186:                 return BigDecimal.class;
      >> 187:             return BigInteger.class;
      >> 188:         }
      >> 189:         if (w2 == BigInteger.class) {
      >> 190:             if (w1 == Float.class || w1 == Double.class)
      >> 191:                 return BigDecimal.class;
      >> 192:             return BigInteger.class;
      >> 193:         }
      >> 194:         if (w1 == Double.class || w2 == Double.class)
      >> 195:             return double.class;
      >> 196:         if (w1 == Float.class || w2 == Float.class)
      >> 197:             return float.class;
      >> 198:         if (w1 == Long.class || w2 == Long.class)
      >> 199:             return long.class;
      >> 200:         return int.class;
      >> 201:     }

      >> 206:     private static boolean isNonstandardType(Class<?> c) {

      >> 223:      * Return whether an instance of the first class can be converted to

      >> 226:     public static boolean canConvert(Class<?> c1, Class<?> c2, boolean strict) {
      >> 227:         if (c1 == c2)
      >> 228:             return true;
      >> 229:         c1 = wrap(c1);
      >> 230:         c2 = wrap(c2);
      >> 231:         if (c2.isAssignableFrom(c1))
      >> 232:             return true;
      >> 233: 
      >> 234:         boolean c1Number = Number.class.isAssignableFrom(c1);
      >> 235:         boolean c2Number = Number.class.isAssignableFrom(c2);
      >> 236:         if (c1Number && c2Number)
      >> 237:             return true;
      >> 238:         if ((c1Number && (c2 == Character.class
      >> 239:             || (!strict && c2 == String.class)))
      >> 240:             || (c2Number && (c1 == Character.class
      >> 241:             || (!strict && c1 == String.class))))
      >> 242:             return true;
      >> 243:         if (c1 == String.class && c2 == Character.class)
      >> 244:             return true;
      >> 245:         if (c2 == String.class)
      >> 246:             return !strict;
      >> 247:         if (c1 == String.class && isTemporalType(c2))
      >> 248:             return true;
      >> 249:         if ((c1 == java.util.Date.class ||c1 == java.sql.Time.class) && c2 == java.sql.Timestamp.class)
      >> 250:             return false;
      >> 251:         if ((c1 == java.util.Date.class ||c1 == java.sql.Timestamp.class) && c2 == java.sql.Time.class)
      >> 252:             return false;
      >> 253:         if (isTemporalType(c1) && isTemporalType(c2))
      >> 254:             return true;
      >> 255:         return false;
      >> 256:     }

      >> 277:     /**
      >> 278:      * Convert the given value to the given type.
      >> 279:      * @param o the given value
      >> 280:      * @param type the target type
      >> 281:      */
      >> 282:     public static Object convert(Object o, Class<?> type, boolean strictNumericConversion) {
      >> 283:         if (o == null)
      >> 284:             return null;
      >> 285:         if (o.getClass() == type)
      >> 286:             return o;
      >> 287: 
      >> 288:         type = wrap(type);
      >> 289:         if (type.isAssignableFrom(o.getClass()))
      >> 290:             return o;
      >> 291: 
      >> 292:         // the non-numeric conversions we do are to string, or from
      >> 293:         // string/char to number, or calendar/date
      >> 294:         // String to Boolean
      >> 295:         // String to Integer
      >> 296:         boolean num = o instanceof Number;
      >> 297:         if (!num) {
      >> 298:             if (type == String.class) {
      >> 299:                 return o.toString();
      >> 300:             }
      >> 301:             else if (type == Boolean.class && o instanceof String) {
      >> 302:                 return Boolean.valueOf(o.toString());
      >> 303:             }
      >> 304:             else if (type == Integer.class && o instanceof String) {
      >> 305:                 try {
      >> 306:                     return new Integer(o.toString());
      >> 307:                 }
      >> 308:                 catch (NumberFormatException e) {
      >> 309:                     throw new ClassCastException(_loc.get("cant-convert", o, o.getClass(), type).getMessage());
      >> 310:                 }
      >> 311:             }
      >> 312:             else if (type == Character.class) {
      >> 313:                 String str = o.toString();
      >> 314:                 if (str != null && str.length() == 1) {
      >> 315:                     return str.charAt(0);
      >> 316:                 }
      >> 317:             }
      >> 318:             else if (Calendar.class.isAssignableFrom(type) && o instanceof Date) {
      >> 319:                 Calendar cal = Calendar.getInstance();
      >> 320:                 cal.setTime((Date) o);
      >> 321:                 return cal;
      >> 322:             }
      >> 323:             else if (Date.class.isAssignableFrom(type) && o instanceof Calendar) {
      >> 324:                 return ((Calendar) o).getTime();
      >> 325:             }
      >> 326:             else if (Number.class.isAssignableFrom(type)) {
      >> 327:                 Integer i = null;
      >> 328:                 if (o instanceof Character) {
      >> 329:                     i = Integer.valueOf((Character) o);
      >> 330:                 }
      >> 331:                 else if (o instanceof String && ((String) o).length() == 1) {
      >> 332:                     i = Integer.valueOf(((String) o));
      >> 333:                 }
      >> 334: 
      >> 335:                 if (i != null) {
      >> 336:                     if (type == Integer.class) {
      >> 337:                         return i;
      >> 338:                     }
      >> 339:                     num = true;
      >> 340:                 }
      >> 341:             } else if (Temporal.class.isAssignableFrom(type)) {
      >> 342:                 // handling of Java8 time API.
      >> 343:                 if (LocalDate.class.equals(type)) {
      >> 344:                     if (o instanceof java.sql.Date) {
      >> 345:                         return ((java.sql.Date) o).toLocalDate();
      >> 346:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {
      >> 347:                         return new java.sql.Date(((java.util.Date)o).getTime()).toLocalDate();
      >> 348:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 349:                         return LocalDate.parse((CharSequence) o);
      >> 350:                     }
      >> 351:                 } else if (LocalDateTime.class.equals(type)) {
      >> 352:                     if (o instanceof java.sql.Timestamp) {
      >> 353:                         return ((java.sql.Timestamp) o).toLocalDateTime();
      >> 354:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {
      >> 355:                         return ((java.util.Date)o).toInstant().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toLocalDateTime();
      >> 356:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 357:                         return LocalDateTime.parse((CharSequence) o);
      >> 358:                     }
      >> 359:                 } else if (LocalTime.class.equals(type)) {
      >> 360:                     if (o instanceof java.sql.Time) {
      >> 361:                         return ((java.sql.Time) o).toLocalTime();
      >> 362:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {
      >> 363:                         return ((java.util.Date)o).toInstant().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toLocalTime();
      >> 364:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 365:                         return LocalTime.parse((CharSequence) o);
      >> 366:                     }
      >> 367:                 } else if (OffsetTime.class.equals(type)) {
      >> 368:                     if (o instanceof java.sql.Time) {
      >> 369:                         return ((java.sql.Time) o).toLocalTime().atOffset(OffsetDateTime.now().getOffset());
      >> 370:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {
      >> 371:                         return ((java.util.Date)o).toInstant().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toOffsetDateTime().toOffsetTime();
      >> 372:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 373:                         return OffsetTime.parse((CharSequence) o);
      >> 374:                     }
      >> 375:                 } else if (OffsetDateTime.class.equals(type)) {
      >> 376:                     if (o instanceof java.sql.Timestamp) {
      >> 377:                         return ((java.sql.Timestamp) o).toInstant().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toOffsetDateTime();
      >> 378:                     } else if (o instanceof java.util.Date) {
      >> 379:                         return ((java.util.Date)o).toInstant().atZone(ZoneId.systemDefault()).toOffsetDateTime();
      >> 380:                     } else if (o instanceof CharSequence) {
      >> 381:                         return OffsetDateTime.parse((CharSequence) o);
      >> 382:                     }
      >> 383:                 }
      >> 384: 
      >> 385:             } else if (o instanceof String && isJDBCTemporalSyntax(o.toString())) {
      >> 386:                 try {
      >> 387:                     Object temporal = parseJDBCTemporalSyntax(o.toString());
      >> 388:                     if (temporal != null && type.isAssignableFrom(temporal.getClass()))
      >> 389:                         return temporal;
      >> 390:                 } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
      >> 391: 
      >> 392:                 }
      >> 393:             } else if (o instanceof String && type.isEnum()) {
      >> 394:                 return Enum.valueOf((Class<Enum>)type, o.toString());
      >> 395:             }
      >> 396:         }
      >> 397:         if (!num) {
      >> 398:             throw new ClassCastException(_loc.get("cant-convert", o, o.getClass(), type).getMessage());
      >> 399:         }
      >> 400: 
      >> 401:         if (type == Integer.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 402:             return ((Number) o).intValue();
      >> 403:         } else if (type == Float.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 404:             return ((Number) o).floatValue();
      >> 405:         } else if (type == Double.class) {
      >> 406:             return ((Number) o).doubleValue();
      >> 407:         } else if (type == Long.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 408:             return ((Number) o).longValue();
      >> 409:         } else if (type == BigDecimal.class) {
      >> 410:             // the BigDecimal constructor doesn't handle the
      >> 411:             // "NaN" string version of Double.NaN and Float.NaN, nor
      >> 412:             // does it handle infinity; we need to instead use the Double
      >> 413:             // and Float versions, despite wanting to cast it to BigDecimal
      >> 414:             double dval = ((Number) o).doubleValue();
      >> 415:             if (Double.isNaN(dval) || Double.isInfinite(dval)) {
      >> 416:                 return dval;
      >> 417:             }
      >> 418:             float fval = ((Number) o).floatValue();
      >> 419:             if (Float.isNaN(fval) || Float.isInfinite(fval)) {
      >> 420:                 return fval;
      >> 421:             }
      >> 422: 
      >> 423:             return new BigDecimal(o.toString());
      >> 424:         } else if (type == BigInteger.class) {
      >> 425:             return new BigInteger(o.toString());
      >> 426:         } else if (type == Short.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 427:             return ((Number) o).shortValue();
      >> 428:         } else if (type == Byte.class && allowNumericConversion(o.getClass(), type, strictNumericConversion)) {
      >> 429:             return ((Number) o).byteValue();
      >> 430:         } else if (type == Character.class) {
      >> 431:             return (char) ((Number) o).intValue();
      >> 432:         } else if (!strictNumericConversion) {
      >> 433:             return ((Number) o).intValue();
      >> 434:         } else {
      >> 435:             throw new ClassCastException(_loc.get("cant-convert", o, o.getClass(), type).getMessage());
      >> 436:         }
      >> 437:     }
      >> 438: 
      >> 439:     private static boolean allowNumericConversion(Class<?> actual, Class<?> target, boolean strict) {
      >> 440:         if (!strict || actual == target)
      >> 441:             return true;
      >> 442:         if (actual == Byte.class)    return false;
      >> 443:         if (actual == Double.class)  return target == Float.class;
      >> 444:         if (actual == Float.class)   return target == Double.class;
      >> 445:         if (actual == Integer.class) return target == Long.class || target == Short.class;
      >> 446:         if (actual == Long.class)    return target == Integer.class || target == Short.class;
      >> 447:         if (actual == Short.class)   return target == Long.class || target == Integer.class;
      >> 448:         return false;
      >> 449:     }

      >> 478: 
      >> 479:     /**
      >> 480:      * Mod the given values.
      >> 481:      */
      >> 482:     public static Object mod(Object o1, Class<?> c1, Object o2, Class<?> c2) {
      >> 483:         return op(o1, c1, o2, c2, OP_MOD);
      >> 484:     }
      >> 485: 
      >> 486:     /**
      >> 487:      * Power the base to the exponent
      >> 488:      */
      >> 489:     public static Object power(Object o1, Class<?> c1, Object o2, Class<?> c2) {
      >> 490:         return op(o1, c1, o2, c2, OP_POWER);
      >> 491:     }
      >> 492: 
      >> 493:     public static Object round(Object o1, Class<?> c1, Object o2, Class<?> c2) {
      >> 494:         return op(o1, c1, o2, c2, OP_ROUND);
      >> 495:     }
      >> 496: 
      >> 497:     /**
      >> 498:      * Perform the given operation on two numbers.
      >> 499:      */
      >> 500:     private static Object op(Object o1, Class<?> c1, Object o2, Class<?> c2, int op) {
      >> 501:         Class<?> promote = promote(c1, c2);
      >> 502:         if (promote == int.class) {
      >> 503:             int n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0 : ((Number) o1).intValue();
      >> 504:             int n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0 : ((Number) o2).intValue();
      >> 505:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 506:         }
      >> 507:         if (promote == float.class) {
      >> 508:             float n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0F : ((Number) o1).floatValue();
      >> 509:             float n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0F : ((Number) o2).floatValue();
      >> 510:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 511:         }
      >> 512:         if (promote == double.class) {
      >> 513:             double n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0D : ((Number) o1).doubleValue();
      >> 514:             double n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0D : ((Number) o2).doubleValue();
      >> 515:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 516:         }
      >> 517:         if (promote == long.class) {
      >> 518:             long n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0L : ((Number) o1).longValue();
      >> 519:             long n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0L : ((Number) o2).longValue();
      >> 520:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 521:         }
      >> 522:         if (promote == BigDecimal.class) {
      >> 523:             BigDecimal n1 = (o1 == null) ? ZERO_BIGDECIMAL
      >> 524:                 : (BigDecimal) convert(o1, promote);
      >> 525:             BigDecimal n2 = (o2 == null) ? ZERO_BIGDECIMAL
      >> 526:                 : (BigDecimal) convert(o2, promote);
      >> 527:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 528:         }
      >> 529:         if (promote == BigInteger.class) {
      >> 530:             BigInteger n1 = (o1 == null) ? ZERO_BIGINTEGER
      >> 531:                 : (BigInteger) convert(o1, promote);
      >> 532:             BigInteger n2 = (o2 == null) ? ZERO_BIGINTEGER
      >> 533:                 : (BigInteger) convert(o2, promote);
      >> 534:             return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 535:         }
      >> 536:         // default to int
      >> 537:         int n1 = (o1 == null) ? 0 : ((Number) o1).intValue();
      >> 538:         int n2 = (o2 == null) ? 0 : ((Number) o2).intValue();
      >> 539:         return op(n1, n2, op);
      >> 540:     }
      >> 541: 
      >> 542:     /**
      >> 543:      * Return the result of a mathematical operation.
      >> 544:      */
      >> 545:     private static Object op(int n1, int n2, int op) {
      >> 546:         int tot;
      >> 547:         switch (op) {
      >> 548:             case OP_ADD:
      >> 549:                 tot = n1 + n2;
      >> 550:                 break;
      >> 551:             case OP_SUBTRACT:
      >> 552:                 tot = n1 - n2;
      >> 553:                 break;
      >> 554:             case OP_MULTIPLY:
      >> 555:                 tot = n1 * n2;
      >> 556:                 break;
      >> 557:             case OP_DIVIDE:
      >> 558:                 tot = n1 / n2;
      >> 559:                 break;
      >> 560:             case OP_MOD:
      >> 561:                 tot = n1 % n2;
      >> 562:                 break;
      >> 563:             case OP_POWER:
      >> 564:                 return Math.pow(n1, n2);
      >> 565:             case OP_ROUND:
      >> 566:                 tot = n1;
      >> 567:             default:
      >> 568:                 throw new InternalException();
      >> 569:         }
      >> 570:         return tot;
      >> 571:     }
      >> 572: 
      >> 573:     /**
      >> 574:      * Return the result of a mathematical operation.
      >> 575:      */
      >> 576:     private static Object op(float n1, float n2, int op) {
      >> 577:         float tot;
      >> 578:         switch (op) {
      >> 579:             case OP_ADD:
      >> 580:                 tot = n1 + n2;
      >> 581:                 break;
      >> 582:             case OP_SUBTRACT:
      >> 583:                 tot = n1 - n2;
      >> 584:                 break;
      >> 585:             case OP_MULTIPLY:
      >> 586:                 tot = n1 * n2;
      >> 587:                 break;
      >> 588:             case OP_DIVIDE:
      >> 589:                 tot = n1 / n2;
      >> 590:                 break;
      >> 591:             case OP_MOD:
      >> 592:                 tot = n1 % n2;
      >> 593:                 break;
      >> 594:             case OP_POWER:
      >> 595:                 return Math.pow(n1, n2);
      >> 596:             case OP_ROUND:
      >> 597:                 BigDecimal bg = new BigDecimal(Float.toString(n1));
      >> 598:                 return bg.setScale(Math.toIntExact(Math.round(n2)), RoundingMode.HALF_EVEN).floatValue();
      >> 599:         default:
      >> 600:                 throw new InternalException();
      >> 601:         }
      >> 602:         return tot;
      >> 603:     }

      >> 668: 
      >> 669:     /**
      >> 670:      * Return the result of a mathematical operation.
      >> 671:      */
      >> 672:     private static Object op(BigDecimal n1, BigDecimal n2, int op) {
      >> 673:         switch (op) {
      >> 674:             case OP_ADD:
      >> 675:                 return n1.add(n2);
      >> 676:             case OP_SUBTRACT:
      >> 677:                 return n1.subtract(n2);
      >> 678:             case OP_MULTIPLY:
      >> 679:                 return n1.multiply(n2);
      >> 680:             case OP_DIVIDE:
      >> 681:                 int scale = Math.max(n1.scale(), n2.scale());
      >> 682:                 return n1.divide(n2, scale, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_UP);
      >> 683:             case OP_MOD:
      >> 684:                 throw new UserException(_loc.get("mod-bigdecimal"));
      >> 685:             case OP_POWER:
      >> 686:                 return n1.pow(n2.intValue());
      >> 687:             case OP_ROUND:
      >> 688:                 return n1.setScale(n2.intValue(), RoundingMode.HALF_EVEN);
      >> 689:             default:
      >> 690:                 throw new InternalException();
      >> 691:         }
      >> 692:     }
      >> 693: 
      >> 694:     /**
      >> 695:      * Return the result of a mathematical operation.
      >> 696:      */
      >> 697:     private static Object op(BigInteger n1, BigInteger n2, int op) {
      >> 698:         switch (op) {
      >> 699:             case OP_ADD:
      >> 700:                 return n1.add(n2);
      >> 701:             case OP_SUBTRACT:
      >> 702:                 return n1.subtract(n2);
      >> 703:             case OP_MULTIPLY:
      >> 704:                 return n1.multiply(n2);
      >> 705:             case OP_DIVIDE:
      >> 706:                 return n1.divide(n2);
      >> 707:             case OP_POWER:
      >> 708:                 return n1.pow(n2.intValue());
      >> 709:             case OP_ROUND:
      >> 710:                 return n1;
      >> 711:             default:
      >> 712:                 throw new InternalException();
      >> 713:         }
      >> 714:     }
      >> 715: 
      >> 716:     /**
      >> 717:      * Parses the given declarations into a list of type, name, type, name...
      >> 718:      * Returns null if no declarations. Assumes declaration is not an empty
      >> 719:      * string and is already trimmed (valid assumptions given the checks made
      >> 720:      * in our setters).
      >> 721:      *
      >> 722:      * @param decType the type of declaration being parsed, for use in
      >> 723:      * error messages
      >> 724:      */
      >> 725:     public static List<String> parseDeclaration(String dec, char split, String decType) {
      >> 726:         if (dec == null)
      >> 727:             return null;
      >> 728: 
      >> 729:         // watch for common mixups between commas and semis
      >> 730:         char bad = (char) 0;
      >> 731:         if (split == ',')
      >> 732:             bad = ';';
      >> 733:         else if (split == ';')
      >> 734:             bad = ',';
      >> 735: 
      >> 736:         char sentinal = ' ';
      >> 737:         char cur;
      >> 738:         int start = 0;
      >> 739:         boolean skipSpace = false;
      >> 740:         List<String> results = new ArrayList<>(6);
      >> 741:         for (int i = 0; i < dec.length(); i++) {
      >> 742:             cur = dec.charAt(i);
      >> 743:             if (cur == bad)
      >> 744:                 throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-dec", dec, decType));
      >> 745:             if (cur == ' ' && skipSpace) {
      >> 746:                 start++;
      >> 747:                 continue;
      >> 748:             }
      >> 749: 
      >> 750:             skipSpace = false;
      >> 751:             if (cur != sentinal)
      >> 752:                 continue;
      >> 753: 
      >> 754:             // if looking for spaces, look for split char, or vice versa
      >> 755:             sentinal = (sentinal == ' ') ? split : ' ';
      >> 756:             results.add(dec.substring(start, i).trim());
      >> 757:             start = i + 1;
      >> 758:             skipSpace = true;
      >> 759:         }
      >> 760: 
      >> 761:         // add last token, if any
      >> 762:         if (start < dec.length())
      >> 763:             results.add(dec.substring(start));
      >> 764: 
      >> 765:         // if not an even number of elements, something is wrong
      >> 766:         if (results.isEmpty() || results.size() % 2 != 0)
      >> 767:             throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-dec", dec, decType));
      >> 768: 
      >> 769:         return results;
      >> 770:     }
      >> 771: 
      >> 772:     /**
      >> 773:      * Split the given expression list into distinct expressions. Assumes the
      >> 774:      * given string is not null or of zero length and is already trimmed
      >> 775:      * (valid assumptions given the checks in our setters and before
      >> 776:      * this method call).
      >> 777:      */
      >> 778:     public static List<String> splitExpressions(String str, char split, int expected) {
      >> 779:         if (str == null)
      >> 780:             return null;
      >> 781: 
      >> 782:         List<String> exps = null;
      >> 783:         int parenDepth = 0;
      >> 784:         int begin = 0, pos = 0;
      >> 785:         boolean escape = false;
      >> 786:         boolean string = false;
      >> 787:         boolean nonspace = false;
      >> 788:         char quote = 0;
      >> 789:         for (char c; pos < str.length(); pos++) {
      >> 790:             c = str.charAt(pos);
      >> 791:             if (c == '\\') {
      >> 792:                 escape = !escape;
      >> 793:                 continue;
      >> 794:             }
      >> 795:             if (escape) {
      >> 796:                 escape = false;
      >> 797:                 continue;
      >> 798:             }
      >> 799: 
      >> 800:             switch (c) {
      >> 801:                 case '\'':
      >> 802:                 case '"':
      >> 803:                     if (string && quote == c)
      >> 804:                         string = false;
      >> 805:                     else if (!string) {
      >> 806:                         quote = c;
      >> 807:                         string = true;
      >> 808:                     }
      >> 809:                     nonspace = true;
      >> 810:                     break;
      >> 811:                 case '(':
      >> 812:                     if (!string)
      >> 813:                         parenDepth++;
      >> 814:                     nonspace = true;
      >> 815:                     break;
      >> 816:                 case ')':
      >> 817:                     if (!string)
      >> 818:                         parenDepth--;
      >> 819:                     nonspace = true;
      >> 820:                     break;
      >> 821:                 case ' ':
      >> 822:                 case '\t':
      >> 823:                 case '\n':
      >> 824:                 case '\r':
      >> 825:                     if (c == split && !string && parenDepth == 0 && nonspace) {
      >> 826:                         if (exps == null)
      >> 827:                             exps = new ArrayList<>(expected);
      >> 828:                         exps.add(str.substring(begin, pos).trim());
      >> 829:                         begin = pos + 1;
      >> 830:                         nonspace = false;
      >> 831:                     }
      >> 832:                     break;
      >> 833:                 default:
      >> 834:                     if (c == split && !string && parenDepth == 0) {
      >> 835:                         if (exps == null)
      >> 836:                             exps = new ArrayList<>(expected);
      >> 837:                         exps.add(str.substring(begin, pos).trim());
      >> 838:                         begin = pos + 1;
      >> 839:                     }
      >> 840:                     nonspace = true;
      >> 841:             }
      >> 842:             escape = false;
      >> 843:         }
      >> 844: 
      >> 845:         if (exps == null) {
      >> 846:             exps = Collections.singletonList(str);
      >> 847:             return exps;
      >> 848:         }
      >> 849: 
      >> 850:         // add last expression and return array
      >> 851:         String last = str.substring(begin).trim();
      >> 852:         if (last.length() > 0)
      >> 853:             exps.add(last);
      >> 854:         return exps;
      >> 855:     }
      >> 856: 
      >> 857:     /**
      >> 858:      * Add the given access path metadatas to the full path list, making sure
      >> 859:      * to maintain only base metadatas in the list. The given list may be null.
      >> 860:      */
      >> 861:     public static List<ClassMetaData> addAccessPathMetaDatas(List<ClassMetaData> metas, ClassMetaData[] path) {
      >> 862:         if (path == null || path.length == 0)
      >> 863:             return metas;
      >> 864: 
      >> 865:         // create set of base class metadatas in access path
      >> 866:         if (metas == null)
      >> 867:             metas = new ArrayList<>();
      >> 868:         int last = metas.size();
      >> 869: 
      >> 870:         // for every element in the path of this executor, compare it
      >> 871:         // to already-gathered elements to see if it should replace
      >> 872:         // a subclass in the list or should be added as a new base;
      >> 873:         // at least it's n^2 of a small n...
      >> 874:         ClassMetaData meta;
      >> 875:         boolean add;
      >> 876:         for (ClassMetaData classMetaData : path) {
      >> 877:             add = true;
      >> 878:             for (int j = 0; add && j < last; j++) {
      >> 879:                 meta = metas.get(j);
      >> 880: 
      >> 881:                 if (meta.getDescribedType().isAssignableFrom(classMetaData.getDescribedType())) {
      >> 882:                     // list already contains base class
      >> 883:                     add = false;
      >> 884:                 }
      >> 885:                 else if (classMetaData.getDescribedType().isAssignableFrom(meta.getDescribedType())) {
      >> 886:                     // this element replaces its subclass
      >> 887:                     add = false;
      >> 888:                     metas.set(j, classMetaData);
      >> 889:                 }
      >> 890:             }
      >> 891: 
      >> 892:             // if no base class of current path element already in
      >> 893:             // list and path element didn't replace a subclass in the
      >> 894:             // list, then add it now as a new base
      >> 895:             if (add)
      >> 896:                 metas.add(classMetaData);
      >> 897:         }
      >> 898:         return metas;
      >> 899:     }
      >> 900: 
      >> 901:     /**
      >> 902:      * Convert the user-given hint value to an aggregate listener.
      >> 903:      * The hint can be an aggregate listener instance or class name.
      >> 904:      */
      >> 905:     public static AggregateListener hintToAggregateListener(Object hint, ClassLoader loader) {
      >> 906:         if (hint == null)
      >> 907:             return null;
      >> 908:         if (hint instanceof AggregateListener)
      >> 909:             return (AggregateListener) hint;
      >> 910: 
      >> 911:         Exception cause = null;
      >> 912:         if (hint instanceof String) {
      >> 913:             try {
      >> 914:                 return (AggregateListener) AccessController.doPrivileged(
      >> 915:                     J2DoPrivHelper.newInstanceAction(Class.forName((String) hint, true, loader)));
      >> 916:             } catch (Exception e) {
      >> 917:                 if (e instanceof PrivilegedActionException)
      >> 918:                     e = ((PrivilegedActionException) e).getException();
      >> 919:                 cause = e;
      >> 920:             }
      >> 921:         }
      >> 922:         throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-agg-listener-hint", hint,
      >> 923:             hint.getClass())).setCause(cause);
      >> 924:     }
      >> 925: 
      >> 926:     /**
      >> 927:      * Convert the user-given hint value to an array of aggregate listeners.
      >> 928:      * The hint can be an aggregate listener, aggregate listener array,
      >> 929:      * collection, or comma-separated class names.
      >> 930:      */
      >> 931:     public static AggregateListener[] hintToAggregateListeners(Object hint, ClassLoader loader) {
      >> 932:         if (hint == null)
      >> 933:             return null;
      >> 934:         if (hint instanceof AggregateListener[])
      >> 935:             return (AggregateListener[]) hint;
      >> 936:         if (hint instanceof AggregateListener)
      >> 937:             return new AggregateListener[]{ (AggregateListener) hint };
      >> 938:         if (hint instanceof Collection) {
      >> 939:             Collection<AggregateListener> c = (Collection<AggregateListener>) hint;
      >> 940:             return c.toArray(new AggregateListener[c.size()]);
      >> 941:         }
      >> 942: 
      >> 943:         Exception cause = null;
      >> 944:         if (hint instanceof String) {
      >> 945:             String[] clss = StringUtil.split((String) hint, ",", 0);
      >> 946:             AggregateListener[] aggs = new AggregateListener[clss.length];
      >> 947:             try {
      >> 948:                 for (int i = 0; i < clss.length; i++)
      >> 949:                     aggs[i] = (AggregateListener) AccessController.doPrivileged(
      >> 950:                         J2DoPrivHelper.newInstanceAction(
      >> 951:                             Class.forName(clss[i], true, loader)));
      >> 952:                 return aggs;
      >> 953:             } catch (Exception e) {
      >> 954:                 if (e instanceof PrivilegedActionException)
      >> 955:                     e = ((PrivilegedActionException) e).getException();
      >> 956:                 cause = e;
      >> 957:             }
      >> 958:         }
      >> 959:         throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-agg-listener-hint", hint,
      >> 960:             hint.getClass())).setCause(cause);
      >> 961:     }
      >> 962: 
      >> 963:     /**

      >> 988: 
      >> 989:     /**
