      >> 994:     public static FilterListener[] hintToFilterListeners(Object hint, ClassLoader loader) {
      >> 995:         if (hint == null)
      >> 996:             return null;
      >> 997:         if (hint instanceof FilterListener[])
      >> 998:             return (FilterListener[]) hint;
      >> 999:         if (hint instanceof FilterListener)
      >> 1000:             return new FilterListener[]{ (FilterListener) hint };
      >> 1001:         if (hint instanceof Collection) {
      >> 1002:             Collection<FilterListener> c = (Collection<FilterListener>) hint;
      >> 1003:             return c.toArray(new FilterListener[c.size()]);
      >> 1004:         }
      >> 1005: 
      >> 1006:         Exception cause = null;
      >> 1007:         if (hint instanceof String) {
      >> 1008:             String[] clss = StringUtil.split((String) hint, ",", 0);
      >> 1009:             FilterListener[] filts = new FilterListener[clss.length];
      >> 1010:             try {
      >> 1011:                 for (int i = 0; i < clss.length; i++)
      >> 1012:                     filts[i] = (FilterListener) AccessController.doPrivileged(
      >> 1013:                         J2DoPrivHelper.newInstanceAction(
      >> 1014:                             Class.forName(clss[i], true, loader)));
      >> 1015:                 return filts;
      >> 1016:             } catch (Exception e) {
      >> 1017:                 if (e instanceof PrivilegedActionException)
      >> 1018:                     e = ((PrivilegedActionException) e).getException();
      >> 1019:                 cause = e;
      >> 1020:             }
      >> 1021:         }
      >> 1022:         throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-filter-listener-hint", hint,
      >> 1023:             hint.getClass())).setCause(cause);
      >> 1024:     }
      >> 1025: 
      >> 1026:     /**
      >> 1027:      * Return the value of the property named by the hint key.
      >> 1028:      */
      >> 1029:     public static Object hintToGetter(Object target, String hintKey) {
      >> 1030:         if (target == null || hintKey == null)
      >> 1031:             return null;
      >> 1032: 
      >> 1033:         Method getter = Reflection.findGetter(target.getClass(), hintKey, true);
      >> 1034:         return Reflection.get(target, getter);
      >> 1035:     }
      >> 1036: 
      >> 1037:     /**
      >> 1038:      * Set the value of the property named by the hint key.
      >> 1039:      */
      >> 1040:     public static void hintToSetter(Object target, String hintKey, Object value) {
      >> 1041:         if (target == null || hintKey == null)
      >> 1042:             return;
      >> 1043: 
      >> 1044:         Method setter = Reflection.findSetter(target.getClass(), hintKey, true);
      >> 1045:         if (value instanceof String) {
      >> 1046:             if ("null".equals(value))
      >> 1047:                 value = null;
      >> 1048:             else {
      >> 1049:                 try {
      >> 1050:                     value = StringUtil.parse((String) value, setter.getParameterTypes()[0]);
      >> 1051:                 } catch (Exception e) {
      >> 1052:                     throw new UserException(_loc.get("bad-setter-hint-arg",
      >> 1053:                         hintKey, value, setter.getParameterTypes()[0])).
      >> 1054:                         setCause(e);
      >> 1055:                 }
      >> 1056:             }
      >> 1057:         }
      >> 1058:         Reflection.set(target, setter, value);
      >> 1059:     }
      >> 1060: 
      >> 1061:     /**
      >> 1062:      * Parses the given string assuming it is a JDBC key expression. Extracts the
      >> 1063:      * data portion and based on the key, calls static java.sql.Date/Time/Timestamp.valueOf(String)
      >> 1064:      * method to convert to a java.sql.Date/Time/Timestamp instance.
      >> 1065:      */
      >> 1066:     public static Object parseJDBCTemporalSyntax(String s) {
      >> 1067:         s = clip(s.trim(), "{", "}", true);
      >> 1068:         if (s.startsWith("ts")) {
      >> 1069:             return java.sql.Timestamp.valueOf(clip(s.substring(2).trim(), "'", "'", false));
      >> 1070:         } else if (s.startsWith("d")) {
      >> 1071:             return java.sql.Date.valueOf(clip(s.substring(1).trim(), "'", "'", false));
      >> 1072:         } else if (s.startsWith("t")) {
      >> 1073:             return java.sql.Time.valueOf(clip(s.substring(2).trim(), "'", "'", false));
      >> 1074:         } else {
      >> 1075:             return null;
      >> 1076:         }
      >> 1077:     }
      >> 1078: 
      >> 1079:     /**
      >> 1080:      * Affirms if the given String is enclosed in {}.
      >> 1081:      *
      >> 1082:      */
      >> 1083:     public static boolean isJDBCTemporalSyntax(String s) {
      >> 1084:         if (s != null) {
      >> 1085:             s = s.trim();
      >> 1086:         }
      >> 1087:         return s != null && s.startsWith("{") && s.endsWith("}");
      >> 1088:     }
      >> 1089: 
      >> 1090:     /**
      >> 1091:      * Removes the first and last string if they are the terminal sequence in the given string.
      >> 1092:      *
      >> 1093:      * @param s a string to be examined
      >> 1094:      * @param first the characters in the beginning of the given string
      >> 1095:      * @param last the characters in the end of the given string
      >> 1096:      * @param fail if true throws exception if the given string does not have the given terminal sequences.
      >> 1097:      * @return the string with terminal sequences removed.
      >> 1098:      */
      >> 1099:     public static String clip(String s, String first, String last, boolean fail) {
      >> 1100:         if (s == null)
      >> 1101:             return s;
      >> 1102:         if (s.startsWith(first) && s.endsWith(last)) {
      >> 1103:             return s.substring(first.length(), s.length()-last.length()).trim();
      >> 1104:         }
      >> 1105:         if (fail) {
      >> 1106:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(s + " is not valid escape syntax for JDBC");
      >> 1107:         }
      >> 1108:         return s;
      >> 1109:     }
      >> 1110: 
      >> 1111:     /**
      >> 1112:      * Affirms if the given class is Data, Time or Timestamp.
      >> 1113:      */
      >> 1114:     public static boolean isTemporalType(Class<?> c) {
      >> 1115:         return c != null
      >> 1116:             && (Date.class.isAssignableFrom(c)
      >> 1117:              || Time.class.isAssignableFrom(c)
      >> 1118:              || Timestamp.class.isAssignableFrom(c)
      >> 1119:              || Calendar.class.isAssignableFrom(c)
      >> 1120:              || LocalDate.class == c  // java.time classes are final, so we can compare with ==
      >> 1121:              || LocalDateTime.class == c
      >> 1122:              || LocalTime.class == c
      >> 1123:              || OffsetTime.class ==c
      >> 1124:              || OffsetDateTime.class == c);
      >> 1125:     }
      >> 1126: 
      >> 1127:     public static Object getDefaultForNull(Class<?> nType) {
      >> 1128:         if (nType == Long.class)
      >> 1129:             return 0L;
      >> 1130:         if (nType == Integer.class)
      >> 1131:             return 0;
      >> 1132:         if (nType == Double.class)
      >> 1133:             return 0.0;
      >> 1134:         if (nType == Float.class)
      >> 1135:             return 0.0F;
      >> 1136:         if (nType == Short.class)
      >> 1137:             return (short) 0;
      >> 1138:         return null;
      >> 1139:     }
      >> 1140: 
      >> 1141: }
